Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the organic space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 200 and the convergence rate is O(802/n^1).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_200(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 200. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^802(S, F) is trivial. QED.
